"\\(\\frac{{217}}{{110}}\\)"}, {"prompt": "2", "value": "\\(\\frac{{281}}{{190}}\\)"}, {"prompt": "3", "value": "\\(\\frac{{289}}{{120}}\\)"}, {"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(\\frac{{512}}{{321}}\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "3", "solution_html": {"en": "\n

Given:

\n\n

\\(\\sin θ = \\frac{8}{{17}}\\), where θ is an acute angle

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1

\n\n

tan θ = \\(\\frac{\\sinθ}{\\cosθ}\\) , and 

\n\n

cot θ = \\(\\frac{\\cosθ}{\\sinθ}\\)

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

In order to find \\(\\tanθ\\) and \\(\\cotθ\\), we need \\(\\cosθ\\):

\n\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1

\n\n

cos θ = \\(\\sqrt{1-{(\\sinθ)}^2} \\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sqrt{1-{(\\frac{8}{17})}^2}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sqrt{1-{(\\frac{64}{289})}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\sqrt{\\frac{225}{289}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\(\\frac{15}{17}\\)

\n\n

Now, calculating \\(\\tan θ + \\cot θ\\):

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{\\sinθ}{\\cosθ}+\\frac{\\cosθ}{\\sinθ}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{(\\sinθ)^2+(\\cosθ)^2}{\\sinθ\\cosθ}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{1}{\\sinθ\\cosθ}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{1}{\\frac{8}{17}\\times\\frac{15}{17}}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{17\\times17}{8\\times15}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\(\\frac{289}{120}\\)

\n\n

 ∴ The value of tan θ  + cot θ is \\(\\bf \\frac{289}{120}\\).

\n\n

Shortcut Trick 

\n\n

\\(\\sin θ = \\frac{8}{{17}}\\) = P/H, we know the Pythagorean triplet (8, 15, 17)

\n\n

Then, base (B) = 15, so tan θ = P/B = 8/15, cot θ = 15/17

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டுள்ளவை:

\n\n

கொடுக்கப்பட்டுள்ளவை:

\n\n

 \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) (sin2 35° + sin2 55°)

\n\n

பயன்படுத்திய கோட்பாடு:

\n\n

1. sin θ = cos (90° - θ)

\n\n

2. tan θ = cot (90° - θ)

\n\n

3. sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

கணக்கீடு: 

\n\n

 \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) (sin2 35° + sin2 55°)

\n\n

⇒  \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) (sin2 35° + cos2 (90°  - 55°)

\n\n

⇒   \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) (sin2 35° + cos2 (35°))

\n\n

⇒  \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) (1)

\n\n

⇒ 4/3

\n\n

∴ சுருங்கிய மதிப்பு 4/3 ஆகும்.

Shortcut Trick\n

Given that sin(θ) = cos(θ).

\n

Divide both sides by cos(θ) to get the ratio.

\n

⇒ sin(θ) ÷ cos(θ) = 1

\n

⇒ tan(θ) = 1

\n

From the standard trigonometric table, we know that tan(45°) = 1.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45°.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n\n\n

Given:

\n

An equation where sin(θ) and cos(θ) are equal.

\n\n

Concept Used:

\n

In a right-angled triangle, if the Perpendicular and Base are equal in length, the corresponding acute angle θ is 45°.

\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Let Perpendicular = 1 unit and Base = 1 unit.

\n

⇒ Hypotenuse = √(12 + 12) = √2 units.

\n

⇒ sin(45°) = Perpendicular ÷ Hypotenuse = 1 ÷ √2

\n

⇒ cos(45°) = Base ÷ Hypotenuse = 1 ÷ √2

\n

⇒ Therefore, at θ = 45°, both trigonometric functions yield the exact same value of 1 ÷ √2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45°.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n\n

Complementary Angles Rule

\n

Since sin(90° − θ) = cos(θ), equating sin(θ) = cos(θ) implies sin(θ) = sin(90° − θ). Solving this gives θ = 90° − θ, which means 2θ = 90° ⇒ θ = 45°.

\n\n

Key Trigonometric Values at 45°

\n

At 45°, both sine and cosine are equal (1 ÷ √2), both tangent and cotangent are equal (1), and both secant and cosecant are equal (√2).

\n\n

Quadrants and Equality

\n

Sine and Cosine are positive and equal at 45° (1st quadrant). They are both negative and equal at 225° (3rd quadrant) where both are −(1 ÷ √2).

\n

⇒ sin(θ) ÷ cos(θ) = 1

\n

⇒ tan(θ) = 1

\n

मानक त्रिकोणमितीय तालिका से, हम जानते हैं कि tan(45°) = 1 है।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 45° है।

{"prompt": "4", "value": "\\(13 \\over12\\)"}], "range": {"start": "", "end": ""}}}, "correct_option": "1", "solution_html": {"en": "\nShortcut Trick

Given cot A = 12/5, which represents Base/Perpendicular ratio.

Using the Pythagorean triplet (5, 12, 13), the Hypotenuse is 13.

sin A = Perpendicular / Hypotenuse = 5 / 13.

∴ The correct answer is 5/13.

Alternate Method

Given: cot A = 12/5

Formula Used: cot A = Base (B) / Perpendicular (P); sin A = Perpendicular (P) / Hypotenuse (H); H2 = P2 + B2

⇒ Let Perpendicular (P) = 5k and Base (B) = 12k

⇒ Hypotenuse (H) = √(P2 + B2)

⇒ H = √((5k)2 + (12k)2) = √(25k2 + 144k2) = √(169k2)

⇒ H = 13k

⇒ sin A = P / H = 5k / 13k = 5/13

∴ The correct answer is 5/13.

Additional Information

Trigonometric Ratios

Standard ratios: sin = P/H, cos = B/H, tan = P/B, cot = B/P, sec = H/B, cosec = H/P.

Pythagorean Identity

Fundamental identity: sin2A + cos2A = 1 and 1 + cot2A = cosec2A.

Common Triplets

Frequently used right-angled triangle sides: (3,4,5), (5,12,13), (8,15,17), (7,24,25).Shortcut Trick

दिया गया है cot A = 12/5, जो आधार/लंब (Base/Perpendicular) अनुपात को दर्शाता है।

पाइथागोरस त्रिक (Pythagorean triplet) (5, 12, 13) का उपयोग करते हुए, कर्ण (Hypotenuse) 13 है।

sin A = लंब / कर्ण = 5 / 13.

∴ सही उत्तर 5/13 है।

Alternate Method

दिया गया है: cot A = 12/5

प्रयुक्त सूत्र: cot A = आधार (B) / लंब (P); sin A = लंब (P) / कर्ण (H); H2 = P2 + B2

⇒ माना लंब (P) = 5k और आधार (B) = 12k

⇒ कर्ण (H) = √(P2 + B2)

⇒ H = √((5k)2 + (12k)2) = √(25k2 + 144k2) = √(169k2)

⇒ H = 13k

⇒ sin A = P / H = 5k / 13k = 5/13

∴ सही उत्तर 5/13 है।

Additional Information

Trigonometric Ratios

मानक अनुपात: sin = P/H, cos = B/H, tan = P/B, cot = B/P, sec = H/B, cosec = H/P.

Pythagorean Identity

मौलिक सर्वसमिका: sin2A + cos2A = 1 और 1 + cot2A = cosec2A.

Common Triplets

अक्सर उपयोग की जाने वाली समकोण त्रिभुज की भुजाएं: (3,4,5), (5,12,13), (8,15,17), (7,24,25)।

वापरलेली संकल्पना:

\n\n

tan x = P/B, जेथे P लंब आहे आणि B पाया आहे.

\n\n

sin x = P/H, जेथे P लंब आहे आणि H कर्ण आहे

\n\n

H 2 = P 2 + B 2

\n\n

गणना:

\n\n

tan x = 10/17

\n\n

P = 10 आणि B = 17

\n\n

H 2 = P 2 + B 2 वापरणे

\n\n

H 2 = 10 2 + 17 2

\n\n

H = √389

\n\n

sin x = P/H

\n\n

sin x = 10/√389

\n\n

म्हणून, sin x चे मूल्य 10/√(389) आहे.

\n82:Tb5a,\n

Shortcut Trick\n

For standard trigonometric angles, we know that sec45° = √2.

\n

Comparing this with the given secθ = √2, we get θ = 45°.

\n

We need to find the value of cosecθ.

\n

Substituting θ = 45° into cosecθ, we get cosec45° = √2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is √2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

secθ = √2

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n

secθ = Hypotenuse / Adjacent

\n

cosecθ = Hypotenuse / Opposite

\n

Opposite = √(Hypotenuse2 − Adjacent2)

\n\n\n\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ secθ = √2 / 1

\n

⇒ Comparing with the formula, Hypotenuse = √2 and Adjacent = 1

\n

⇒ By Pythagoras Theorem: Opposite = √((√2)2 − 12)

\n

⇒ Opposite = √(2 − 1) = √1 = 1

\n

⇒ cosecθ = Hypotenuse / Opposite

\n

⇒ cosecθ = √2 / 1 = √2

\n

∴ The correct answer is √2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Reciprocal Identities

\n

sinθ = 1 / cosecθ | cosθ = 1 / secθ | tanθ = 1 / cotθ

\n

Trigonometric Values for 45°

\n

sin45° = 1/√2 | cos45° = 1/√2 | tan45° = 1

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

sin2θ + cos2θ = 1 | 1 + tan2θ = sec2θ | 1 + cot2θ = cosec2θ

Shortcut Trick\n

मानक त्रिकोणमितीय कोणों के लिए, हम जानते हैं कि sec45° = √2 है।

\n

दिए गए secθ = √2 के साथ इसकी तुलना करने पर, हमें θ = 45° प्राप्त होता है।

\n

हमें cosecθ का मान ज्ञात करना है।

\n

cosecθ में θ = 45° रखने पर, हमें cosec45° = √2 प्राप्त होता है।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर √2 है।

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

दिया गया है:

\n

secθ = √2

\n\n

प्रयुक्त सूत्र:

\n

secθ = कर्ण / आधार

\n

cosecθ = कर्ण / लंब

\n

लंब = √(कर्ण2 − आधार2)

\n\n\n\n

गणना:

\n

⇒ secθ = √2 / 1

\n

⇒ सूत्र के साथ तुलना करने पर, कर्ण = √2 और आधार = 1

\n

⇒ पाइथागोरस प्रमेय द्वारा: लंब = √((√2)2 − 12)

\n

⇒ लंब = √(2 − 1) = √1 = 1

\n

⇒ cosecθ = कर्ण / लंब

\n

⇒ cosecθ = √2 / 1 = √2

\n

∴ सही उत्तर √2 है।

\n\n

Given sin θ − cos θ = 0, which implies sin θ = cos θ.

\n

Since the values are equal, their cubes sin3 θ and cos3 θ will also be equal.

\n

Therefore, the difference sin3 θ − cos3 θ must be zero.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given: sin θ − cos θ = 0

\n

Formula Used: a3 − b3 = (a − b)(a2 + ab + b2)

\n
\n

⇒ sin θ − cos θ = 0

\n

⇒ sin θ = cos θ

\n

⇒ tan θ = 1 ⇒ θ = 45°

\n

⇒ Required value = sin3 45° − cos3 45°

\n

⇒ (1/√2)3 − (1/√2)3

\n

⇒ 1/(2√2) − 1/(2√2) = 0

\n

∴ The correct answer is 0.

\n

Additional Information

\n

Trigonometric Identity

\n

The fundamental identity is sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1.

\n

\n

For any expression (an − bn), if (a − b) = 0, then the entire expression evaluates to 0.

\n

\n

At θ = 45°, sin θ = cos θ = 1/√2. Shortcut Trick\n

\n\n

⇒ B2/B2 = 1

\n\n

विकल्प 1 सही है।

\n\n

विकल्प 2:

\n\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

⇒ (P/H)2 + (B/H)2 = 1

\n\n

⇒ (P2 + B2)/H2

\n\n

⇒ H2/H2 = 1

\n\n

विकल्प 2 सही है।

\n\n

विकल्प 3:

\n\n

cosec2 θ - cot2 θ = 1

\n\n

⇒ (H/P)2 - (B/P)2

\n\n

⇒ (H2 - B2)/P2

\n\n

⇒ P2/P2 = 1

\n\n

विकल्प 3 सही है।

\n\n

विकल्प 4:

\n\n

sec2 θ + cos2 θ = 1

\n\n

⇒ (H/B)2 + (B/H)2 

\n\n

⇒ (H2 + B2)/B2H2 ≠ 1

\n\n

विकल्प 4 गलत है।

\n\n

∴ सही विकल्प 4 है।

Shortcut Trick\n

Given that sin(θ) = cos(θ).

\n

Divide both sides by cos(θ) to get the ratio.

\n

⇒ sin(θ) ÷ cos(θ) = 1

\n

⇒ tan(θ) = 1

\n

From the standard trigonometric table, we know that tan(45°) = 1.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45°.

Shortcut Trick\n

If x + 1/x = 2, then the value of x is always 1.

\n

Since cotA is the reciprocal of tanA, the equation tanA + 1/tanA = 2 implies tanA = 1.

\n

This means cotA = 1/1 = 1.

\n

Substitute these values directly: tan2 A + cot2 A = 12 + 12 = 2.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

tanA + cotA = 2

\n

Formula Used:

\n

(a + b)2 = a2 + b2 + 2ab

\n

tanA × cotA = 1

\n

Calculations:

\n

⇒ Squaring both sides of the given equation:

\n

⇒ (tanA + cotA)2 = 22

\n

⇒ tan2 A + cot2 A + 2 × tanA × cotA = 4

\n

⇒ tan2 A + cot2 A + 2 × 1 = 4

\n

⇒ tan2 A + cot2 A = 4 − 2

\n

⇒ tan2 A + cot2 A = 2

\n

∴ The correct answer is 2.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Higher Powers Identity

\n

If x + 1/x = 2, then for any positive integer n, the value of xn + 1/xn will always be equal to 2.

\n

Squaring Concept for Variables

\n

If x + 1/x = k, then the sum of their squares is derived directly as x2 + 1/x2 = k2 − 2.

\n

Basic Trigonometric Conversions

\n

tanA = sinA ÷ cosA and cotA = cosA ÷ sinA, meaning that tanA and cotA are always reciprocals of each other.

Shortcut Trick\n

यदि x + 1/x = 2 है, तो x का मान हमेशा 1 होता है।

\n

चूँकि cotA, tanA का व्युत्क्रम है, समीकरण tanA + 1/tanA = 2 का तात्पर्य है कि tanA = 1 है।

\n

इसका अर्थ है cotA = 1/1 = 1 है।

\n

इन मानों को सीधे प्रतिस्थापित करें: tan2 A + cot2 A = 12 + 12 = 2.

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 2 है।

Given:

\n\n

Tan θ = 8/15

\n\n

Formula used:

\n\n

By Pythagoras theorem:

\n\n

H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

Tan θ = P/B

\n\n

Sin θ = P/H

\n\n

Where, H = hypotenuse

\n\n

P = perpendicular ; B = base

\n\n

Calculation:

\n\n

Tan θ = 8/15 = P/B

\n\n

By Pythagoras theorem:

\n\n

⇒ H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

⇒ H2 = 82 + 152

\n\n

⇒ H = √(64 + 225) = √289

\n\n

⇒ H = 17

\n\n

\n\n

\\(√{\\frac{1-\\sin\\theta}{1+\\sin\\theta}}~\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ √[{9/17}/{25/17}]

\n\n

⇒ √[9/25] = 3/5

\n\n

∴ The correct answer is \\(\\frac{3}{5}\\).

ସମୀକରଣରେ ମୂଲ୍ୟଗୁଡ଼ିକୁ ରଖିବା:

\n\n

⇒ √[{1 - (8/17)}/{1 + (8/17)}]

\n\n

⇒ √[{9/17}/{25/17}]

\n\n

⇒ √[9/25] = 3/5

\n\n

∴ ସଠିକ୍ ଉତ୍ତର ହେଉଛି \\(\\frac{3}{5}\\).

\n\nd3:T6f0,\n

Given: 

\n\n

tan θ  = 3 / 4

\n\n

Formula Used:

\n\n

Tan θ = P/B

\n\n

sin  Shortcut Trick\n

By using the value substitution method from the standard angle values:

\n

Let θ = 45°, then tan 45° = 1 and cot 45° = 1.

\n

Substitute the values in the equation: tan θ + cot θ = 1 + 1 = 2.

\n

This strictly satisfies the given condition, hence θ = 45°.

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45°.

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n

Given:

\n

tan θ + cot θ = 2

\n

Formula Used:

\n

cot θ = 1 / tan θ

\n

Calculation:

\n

⇒ tan θ + 1 / tan θ = 2

\n

⇒ (tan2 θ + 1) / tan θ = 2

\n

⇒ tan2 θ + 1 = 2 tan θ

\n

⇒ tan2 θ − 2 tan θ + 1 = 0

\n

⇒ (tan θ − 1)2 = 0

\n

⇒ tan θ − 1 = 0

\n

⇒ tan θ = 1

\n

⇒ tan θ = tan 45°

\n

⇒ θ = 45°

\n

∴ The correct answer is 45°.

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

Reciprocal Trigonometric Identities

\n

tan θ = 1 / cot θ, sin θ = 1 / csc θ, and cos θ = 1 / sec θ.

\n

Pythagorean Identities

\n

sin2 θ + cos2 θ = 1, sec2 θ − tan2 θ = 1, and csc2 θ − cot2 θ = 1.

\n

Standard Angle Values

\n

tan 30° = 1 / √3, tan 45° = 1, and tan 60° = √3.

Shortcut Trick\n

मानक कोण मानों से मान प्रतिस्थापन विधि का उपयोग करके:

\n

माना θ = 45°, तो tan 45° = 1 और cot 45° = 1।

\n

समीकरण में मान प्रतिस्थापित करें: tan θ + cot θ = 1 + 1 = 2।

\n

यह दी गई शर्त को पूरी तरह से संतुष्ट करता है, इसलिए θ = 45°।

\n

∴ सही उत्तर 45° है।

\n\n

\n

\n

\n

\n\n

Additional Information

\n

व्युत्क्रम त्रिकोणमितीय सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n

\n

पायथागोरस सर्वसमिकाएँ

\n\n

tan θ = 4/3 = P/B

\n\n

⇒ P = 4 અને B = 3

\n\n

H માટે પાયથાગોરિયન નિયમ લાગુ કરો

\n\n

⇒ H2 = P2 + B2

\n\n

⇒ H2 = 42 + 32 = 16 + 9

\n\n

⇒ H = 5

\n\n

⇒ sin θ = P/H = 4/5

\n\n

⇒ cos θ = B/H = 3/5

\n\n

⇒ 7 sin θ - 2 cos θ = 7 × 4/5 - 2 × 3/5

\n\n

⇒ 28/5 - 6/5 = 22/5

\n\n

∴ 7 sin θ - 2 cos θ = 22/5

Given:- sec2 θ = \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\)

\n\n

To find:- cosec (θ + 30°)

\n\n

Formula used:- sec θ = \\(\\frac{Hypotenuse}{Base}\\) ,  Cosec θ =  \\(\\frac{Hypotenuse}{Height}\\)

\n\n

calculation:- It is given that sec2 θ = \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\)

\n\n

On taking square root both sides,

\n\n

⇒sec θ = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

Since θ is an acute angle then In a right-angled triangle, 

\n\n

Base = \\(\\sqrt3\\) unit and hypotenuse = 2 units

\n\n

Now by using the Pythagorean theorem in a right triangle,

\n\n

⇒Hypotenuse2 = Base2 + height2

\n\n

⇒ 22 = (\\(\\sqrt3\\))2 + height2

\n\n

⇒Height = 1 unit

\n\n

Since Cosec θ =  \\(\\frac{Hypotenuse}{Height}\\)

\n\n

⇒ Cosec θ =  \\(\\frac{2}{1}\\) = 2

\n\n

⇒ Sin θ = \\(\\frac{Height}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

⇒Cos θ = \\(\\frac{Base}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\)

\n\n

Now by using the conversion of Cosec θ , cosec θ = \\({1\\over \\sinθ}\\) 

\n\n

⇒cosec (θ + 30°) = \\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\)

\n\n

Using the formula, sin(a + b) = sin a cos b + cos a sin b

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\sin30^{\\circ}.\\cosθ\\ +\\ \\cos30^{\\circ}.sinθ}\\)

\n\n

using the standard value of sin 30° and cos 30° as \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) and \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) respectively, and using Sin θ and Cos θ by  \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) and \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) respectively,

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\frac{1}{2}.\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\ +\\ \\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}.\\frac{1}{2}}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\) = \\(\\frac{4}{2\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\) = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

The value of cosec (θ + 30°) is \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\) 

\n\n

Alternate Method

\n\n

It is given that sec2 θ =  \\(\\frac{4}{3}\\) then ⇒ sec θ = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\) ⇒ sec 30° = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\), so θ = 30° 

\n\n

Now, cosec (θ + 30°) ⇒ cosec (30° + 30°) ⇒ cosec 60° = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

सूत्र वापरून, sin(a + b) = sin a cos b + cos a sin b

\n\n

⇒ \\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\sin30^{\\circ}.\\cos\\theta\\ +\\ \\cos30^{\\circ}. पाप\\theta}\\)

\n\n

अनुक्रमे sin 30° आणि cos 30° चे मानक मूल्य \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) आणि \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) म्हणून वापरल्यास आणि Sin θ आणि Cos θ अनुक्रमे \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) आणि \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) वापरल्यास, 

\n\n

⇒ \\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\frac{1}{2}.\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\ +\\ \\frac {\\sqrt3}{2}.\\frac{1}{2}}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{4}{2\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

⇒ \\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

cosec चे मूल्य (θ + 30°) \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)आहे.

\n\n

যেহেতু θ সূক্ষ্ম কোণ তাই একটি সমকোণী ত্রিভুজে, 

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

⇒ Sin θ = \\(\\frac{Height}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

⇒Cos θ = \\(\\frac{Base}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\)

\n\n

এখন Cosec θ-এর রূপান্তর ব্যবহার করে পাই, cosec θ =  

\n\n

⇒cosec (θ + 30°) = \\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\)

\n\n

sin(a + b) = sin a cos b + cos a sin b সূত্র ব্যবহার করে

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+θ)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\sin30^{\\circ}.\\cosθ\\ +\\ \\cos30^{\\circ}.sinθ}\\)

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

\n\n

இப்போது பித்தகோரியன் தேற்றத்தை ஒரு செங்கோண முக்கோணத்தில் பயன்படுத்துவதன் மூலம்,

\n\n

\n\n

Cosec θ = \\(\\frac{Hypotenuse}{Height}\\)

\n\n

⇒ Cosec θ =  \\(\\frac{2}{1}\\) = 2

\n\n

⇒ Sin θ = \\(\\frac{Height}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\)

\n\n

⇒Cos θ = \\(\\frac{Base}{Hypotenuse}\\) = \\(\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\)

\n\n

இப்போது Cosec θ இன் மாற்றத்தைப் பயன்படுத்தி, cosec θ = \\({1\\over \\sin\\theta}\\)

\n\n

⇒ cosec (θ + 30°) = \\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\)

\n\n

சூத்திரத்தைப் பயன்படுத்தி, sin(a + b) = sin a cos b + cos a sin b

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\sin30^{\\circ}.\\cos\\theta\\ +\\ \\cos30^{\\circ}.sin\\theta}\\)

\n\n

sin 30° மற்றும் cos 30° இன் நிலையான மதிப்பை முறையே \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) மற்றும் \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) ஆகப் பயன்படுத்தி, மற்றும் sin θ மற்றும் Cos θ \\(\\frac{1}{2}\\) மற்றும் \\(\\frac{\\sqrt{3}}{2}\\) முறையே,

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{1}{\\frac{1}{2}.\\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}\\ +\\ \\frac{\\sqrt3}{2}.\\frac{1}{2}}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{4}{2\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

⇒\\({1\\over \\sin(30+\\theta)}\\) = \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n

cosec இன் மதிப்பு (θ + 30°) \\(\\frac{2}{\\sqrt3}\\)

\n\n52:T566,\n

Calculations:

\n\n

According to question,

\n\n

Sin θ = 3/5

\n\n

Then, tan θ

\n\n

= Sin θ/[√(1 - sin²θ)]

\n\n

= (3/5)/[√(1 - (3/5)²)]

\n\n

= (3/5)/√(16/25)

\n\n

= (3/5)/(4/5)

\n\n

= 3/4

\n\n

Hence, The Required value is 3/4.
